   513	    override fun onApplyWindowInsets(insets: WindowInsets): WindowInsets {
   514	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   515	        return super.onApplyWindowInsets(insets)
   516	    }
   517	
   518	    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
   519	        super.onDraw(canvas)
   520	
   521	        val progress = bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis)
   522	        val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
   523	            progress,
   524	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
   525	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
   526	        )
   527	        val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2500, 3900)
   528	        drawIntroBackground(canvas, blackToPurple, purpleToSettle)
   529	
   530	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return
   531	        finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded()
   532	        finishMockFlowSwitchIfNeeded()
   533	        finishMockCreateAvatarCarouselIfNeeded()
   534	        finishMockForgotPasswordSubmissionIfNeeded()
   535	        val mockProgress = mockSignInProgress()
   536	        updateMockFormFocusAnimation()
   537	        updateMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   538	        updateMockInputDimAnimation()
   539	        val formFocusMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress)
   540	
   541	        val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(progress, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
   542	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
   543	            drawSettlingLogo(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, progress, logoAlpha, formFocusMotion)
   544	        }
   545	
   546	        val avatarAlpha = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   547	        val mockMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockProgress)
   548	        if (avatarAlpha > 0.01f) {
   549	            val stackAlpha = avatarAlpha * (1f - mockMotion)
   550	            val y = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha) + MOCK_SIGN_IN_STACK_EXIT_Y * mockMotion
   551	            val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
   552	            if (stackAlpha > 0.01f) {
   553	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveAvatar, Bounds(152f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   554	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinAvatar, Bounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   555	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyAvatar, Bounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   556	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestAvatar, Bounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   557	
   558	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveName, Bounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   559	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinName, Bounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   560	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyName, Bounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   561	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestName, Bounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 200f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   562	            }
   563	        }
   564	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   565	            val outgoingFlow = outgoingMockFlow
   566	            if (outgoingFlow != null) {
   567	                val switchMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFlowSwitchProgress())
   568	                drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
   569	                    canvas,
   570	                    outgoingFlow,
   571	                    stage.left,
   572	                    stage.top,
   573	                    stage.scale,
   574	                    1f - switchMotion,
   575	                    formFocusMotion,
   576	                    updateLabelAnimation = false
   577	                )
   578	                activeMockFlow?.let { incomingFlow ->
   579	                    drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
   580	                        canvas,
   581	                        incomingFlow,
   582	                        stage.left,
   583	                        stage.top,
   584	                        stage.scale,
   585	                        switchMotion,
   586	                        formFocusMotion,
   587	                        updateLabelAnimation = true
   588	                    )
   589	                }
   590	            } else {
   591	                drawMockSignInForm(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, mockMotion, formFocusMotion)
   592	            }
   593	            drawBackChevron(
   594	                canvas,
   595	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X,
   596	                mockBackChevronRowTopY(),
   597	                stage.left,
   598	                accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale),
   599	                stage.scale,
   600	                mockMotion
   601	            )
   602	        }
   603	        drawAccountPrompt(
   604	            canvas = canvas,
   605	            stageLeft = stage.left,
   606	            stageTop = accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale),
   607	            stageScale = stage.scale,
   608	            yOffset = if (mockSignInStartMillis == null) {
   609	                lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha)
   610	            } else {
   611	                0f
   612	            },
   613	            alpha = avatarAlpha
   614	        )
   615	
   616	        if (
   617	            progress < 1f ||
   618	            isMockSignInAnimating() ||
   619	            isMockFlowSwitchAnimating() ||
   620	            isMockFormLabelAnimating() ||
   621	            isMockFormFocusAnimating() ||
   622	            isMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimating() ||
   623	            isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating() ||
   624	            isMockForgotPasswordSubmissionAnimating() ||
   625	            isMockInputDimAnimating()
   626	        ) {
   627	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   628	        }
   629	    }
   630	
   631	    override fun onTouchEvent(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
   632	        return when (event.actionMasked) {
   633	            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
   634	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   635	                    mockTouchDownX = event.x
   636	                    mockTouchDownY = event.y
   637	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   638	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   639	                    if (isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()) {
   640	                        return true
   641	                    }
   642	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   643	                    pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   644	                        settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   645	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   646	                        !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   647	                        settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   648	                    if (
   649	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   650	                        pressedSignInPrompt ||
   651	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt
   652	                    ) {
   653	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   654	                        return true
   655	                    }
   656	                    val touchedDropdownOptions = mockDropdownOptionsHit(event.x, event.y)
   657	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = touchedDropdownOptions?.takeIf { mockDropdownCanScroll(it) }
   658	                    mockDropdownScrollStartY = event.y
   659	                    mockDropdownScrollStartOffset = activeMockDropdownScroll?.let { mockDropdownScrollOffset(it) } ?: 0
   660	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = if (touchedDropdownOptions != null) {
   661	                        mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   662	                    } else {
   663	                        null
   664	                    }
   665	                    pressedMockDropdown = if (pressedMockDropdownOption == null && touchedDropdownOptions == null) {
   666	                        mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   667	                    } else {
   668	                        null
   669	                    }
   670	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = if (
   671	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   672	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   673	                        pressedMockDropdown == null
   674	                    ) {
   675	                        mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   676	                    } else {
   677	                        null
   678	                    }
   679	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   680	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   681	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   682	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   683	                        mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   684	                    val touchedCreateAvatarCarousel = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   685	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   686	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   687	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   688	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   689	                        mockCreateAvatarCarouselHit(event.x, event.y) &&
   690	                        !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
   691	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   692	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   693	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   694	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = if (
   695	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   696	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   697	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   698	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   699	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   700	                        touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   701	                    ) {
   702	                        mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   703	                    } else {
   704	                        null
   705	                    }
   706	                    pressedMockField = if (
   707	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   708	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   709	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   710	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   711	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   712	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   713	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   714	                    ) {
   715	                        mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   716	                    } else {
   717	                        null
   718	                    }
   719	                    pressedRememberMe = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   720	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   721	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   722	                        pressedMockField == null &&
   723	                        mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   724	                    pressedBackChevron = pressedMockField == null &&
   725	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   726	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   727	                        !pressedRememberMe &&
   728	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   729	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   730	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   731	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   732	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   733	                        mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   734	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   735	                    return true
   736	                }
   737	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   738	                pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   739	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   740	                    !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   741	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   742	                pressedAvatarProfile = if (
   743	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   744	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   745	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt
   746	                ) {
   747	                    null
   748	                } else {
   749	                    settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
   750	                }
   751	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   752	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   753	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   754	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null
   755	            }
   756	            MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
   757	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   758	                    if (updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event)) {
   759	                        return true
   760	                    }
   761	                    if (updateMockDropdownScroll(event)) {
   762	                        return true
   763	                    }
   764	                    if (mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) == null && shouldStartMockDragReturn(event)) {
   765	                        mockDragReturnInProgress = true
   766	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
   767	                    }
   768	                    return true
   769	                }
   770	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   771	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   772	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   773	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null
   774	            }
   775	            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
   776	                if (mockDragReturnInProgress) {
   777	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   778	                    pressedMockField = null
   779	                    pressedRememberMe = false
   780	                    pressedBackChevron = false
   781	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   782	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   783	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   784	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   785	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   786	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   787	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   788	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   789	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   790	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   791	                    return true
   792	                }
   793	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   794	                    if (isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()) {
   795	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   796	                        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   797	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   798	                        return true
   799	                    }
   800	                    val requestedFlow = when {
   801	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt && settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   802	                            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
   803	                        }
   804	                        pressedSignInPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   805	                            MockAccountFlow.SignIn
   806	                        }
   807	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt && settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   808	                            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
   809	                        }
   810	                        else -> null
   811	                    }
   812	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   813	                    pressedSignInPrompt = false
   814	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   815	                    if (requestedFlow != null) {
   816	                        super.performClick()
   817	                        switchMockAccountFlow(requestedFlow)
   818	                        return true
   819	                    }
   820	                    val releasedDropdownOption = mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   821	                    val releasedDropdown = mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   822	                    val releasedField = mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   823	                    val releasedRememberMe = mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   824	                    val releasedBackChevron = mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   825	                    val releasedCreateAvatarNav = mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   826	                    val releasedForgotRecoveryTarget = mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   827	                    val releasedForgotPasswordSubmit = mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   828	                    val wasTouchingDropdownOptions = activeMockDropdownScroll != null ||
   829	                        mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) != null
   830	                    val wasScrollingDropdown = mockDropdownScrollMoved
   831	                    val wasPressingRememberMe = pressedRememberMe
   832	                    val wasPressingBackChevron = pressedBackChevron
   833	                    val wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget != null
   834	                    val wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit
   835	                    val wasDraggingCreateAvatar = activeMockCreateAvatarDrag
   836	                    val createAvatarDragMoved = mockCreateAvatarDragMoved
   837	                    val createAvatarDragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
   838	                    val createAvatarDragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress()
   839	                    val pressedDropdown = pressedMockDropdown
   840	                    val pressedOption = pressedMockDropdownOption
   841	                    val pressedCreateAvatarNav = pressedMockCreateAvatarNav
   842	                    val pressedForgotRecoverySelection = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget
   843	                    val shouldSelectDropdownOption = !wasScrollingDropdown &&
   844	                        pressedOption != null &&
   845	                        pressedOption == releasedDropdownOption
   846	                    val shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null &&
   847	                        pressedForgotRecoverySelection == releasedForgotRecoveryTarget
   848	                    val shouldToggleDropdown = pressedDropdown != null && pressedDropdown == releasedDropdown
   849	                    val shouldToggleRememberMe = wasPressingRememberMe && releasedRememberMe
   850	                    val shouldCloseMockSignIn = wasPressingBackChevron && releasedBackChevron
   851	                    val shouldSubmitForgotPassword = wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit && releasedForgotPasswordSubmit
   852	                    val shouldNavigateCreateAvatar = pressedCreateAvatarNav != null &&
   853	                        !createAvatarDragMoved &&
   854	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == releasedCreateAvatarNav
   855	                    val shouldSwipeCreateAvatar = wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
   856	                        createAvatarDragMoved &&
   857	                        createAvatarDragDirection != null &&
   858	                        createAvatarDragProgress >= MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_DRAG_COMMIT_PROGRESS
   859	                    val shouldFocusField = releasedField != null &&
   860	                        !wasTouchingDropdownOptions &&
   861	                        !wasPressingRememberMe &&
   862	                        !wasPressingBackChevron &&
   863	                        !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget &&
   864	                        !wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   865	                        !wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
   866	                        pressedDropdown == null &&
   867	                        pressedOption == null &&
   868	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   869	                        (pressedMockField == null || pressedMockField == releasedField)
   870	                    pressedMockField = null
   871	                    pressedRememberMe = false
   872	                    pressedBackChevron = false
   873	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   874	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   875	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   876	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   877	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   878	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   879	                    this.pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   880	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   881	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   882	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   883	                    super.performClick()
   884	                    if (shouldCloseMockSignIn) {
   885	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
   886	                    } else if (shouldSwipeCreateAvatar && createAvatarDragDirection != null) {
   887	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   888	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   889	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(createAvatarDragDirection, createAvatarDragProgress)
   890	                    } else if (shouldNavigateCreateAvatar && pressedCreateAvatarNav != null) {
   891	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   892	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   893	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(pressedCreateAvatarNav)
   894	                    } else if (wasDraggingCreateAvatar) {
   895	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   896	                    } else if (shouldSelectDropdownOption && pressedOption != null) {
   897	                        setMockDropdownValue(pressedOption)
   898	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   899	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   900	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   901	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   902	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   903	                    } else if (shouldToggleDropdown && pressedDropdown != null) {
   904	                        expandedMockDropdown = if (expandedMockDropdown == pressedDropdown) null else pressedDropdown
   905	                        expandedMockDropdown?.let { prepareMockDropdownScroll(it) }
   906	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   907	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   908	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   909	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   910	                    } else if (shouldToggleRememberMe) {
   911	                        rememberMeChecked = !rememberMeChecked
   912	                        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
   913	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   914	                    } else if (shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget && pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null) {
   915	                        setMockForgotRecoveryTarget(pressedForgotRecoverySelection)
   916	                    } else if (shouldSubmitForgotPassword) {
   917	                        startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
   918	                    } else if (shouldFocusField && releasedField != null) {
   919	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   920	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   921	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   922	                        focusMockSignInField(releasedField)
   923	                    } else if (wasTouchingDropdownOptions || wasScrollingDropdown) {
   924	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   925	                    } else if (!wasPressingRememberMe && !wasPressingBackChevron && !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget) {
   926	                        if (expandedMockDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
   927	                            expandedMockDropdown = null
   928	                            restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   929	                            restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   930	                        }
   931	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   932	                    } else {
   933	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   934	                    }
   935	                    return true
   936	                }
   937	                val shouldOpenMockCreateAccount = pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   938	                    settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   939	                val shouldOpenMockSignIn = pressedSignInPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   940	                val shouldOpenMockForgotPassword = pressedForgotPasswordPrompt &&
   941	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   942	                val releasedAvatarProfile = settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
   943	                val shouldNavigate = pressedAvatarProfile != null && pressedAvatarProfile == releasedAvatarProfile
   944	                clickAvatarProfile = pressedAvatarProfile
   945	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
   946	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   947	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
   948	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   949	                if (shouldOpenMockCreateAccount) {
   950	                    super.performClick()
   951	                    openMockCreateAccountScreen()
   952	                    true
   953	                } else if (shouldOpenMockSignIn) {
   954	                    super.performClick()
   955	                    openMockSignInScreen()
   956	                    true
   957	                } else if (shouldOpenMockForgotPassword) {
   958	                    super.performClick()
   959	                    openMockForgotPasswordScreen()
   960	                    true
   961	                } else if (shouldNavigate) {
   962	                    performClick()
   963	                    true
   964	                } else {
   965	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
   966	                    false
   967	                }
   968	            }
   969	            MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
   970	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
   971	                clickAvatarProfile = null
   972	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   973	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
   974	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   975	                pressedMockField = null
   976	                pressedRememberMe = false
   977	                pressedBackChevron = false
   978	                pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   979	                activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   980	                mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   981	                mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   982	                pressedMockDropdown = null
   983	                pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   984	                pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   985	                pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   986	                activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   987	                mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   988	                mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   989	                false
   990	            }
   991	            else -> pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   992	                pressedSignInPrompt ||
   993	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   994	                pressedAvatarProfile != null ||
   995	                mockSignInStartMillis != null
   996	        }
   997	    }
   998	
   999	    override fun onCheckIsTextEditor(): Boolean {
  1000	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && !isMockSignInClosing()
  1001	    }
  1002	
  1003	    override fun onCreateInputConnection(outAttrs: EditorInfo): InputConnection? {
  1004	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return null
  1005	        outAttrs.inputType = when (field) {
  1006	            MockSignInField.Password,
  1007	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_PASSWORD
  1008	            MockSignInField.Email -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_EMAIL_ADDRESS
  1009	            MockSignInField.Month,
  1010	            MockSignInField.Day,
  1011	            MockSignInField.Year -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_NUMBER
  1012	            else -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_NORMAL
  1013	        }
  1014	        outAttrs.imeOptions = if (field == activeMockFields().lastOrNull()) {
  1015	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE
  1016	        } else {
  1017	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_NEXT
  1018	        }
  1019	        val textLength = mockSignInFieldText(field).length
  1020	        outAttrs.initialSelStart = textLength
  1021	        outAttrs.initialSelEnd = textLength
  1022	        return MockSignInInputConnection()
  1023	    }
  1024	
  1025	    override fun onKeyDown(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  1026	        if (focusedMockField != null) {
  1027	            return handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode, event) || super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
  1028	        }
  1029	        return super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
  1030	    }
  1031	
  1032	    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
  1033	    override fun onKeyMultiple(keyCode: Int, repeatCount: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  1034	        val characters = event.getCharacters()
  1035	        if (focusedMockField != null && !characters.isNullOrEmpty()) {
  1036	            return appendMockSignInInput(characters)
  1037	        }
  1038	        return super.onKeyMultiple(keyCode, repeatCount, event)
  1039	    }
  1040	
  1041	    override fun performClick(): Boolean {
  1042	        super.performClick()
  1043	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return false
  1044	        val profile = clickAvatarProfile ?: return false
  1045	        onAvatarSelected?.invoke(profile)
  1046	        clickAvatarProfile = null
  1047	        return true
  1048	    }
  1049	
  1050	    private fun drawSettlingLogo(
  1051	        canvas: Canvas,
  1052	        stageLeft: Float,
  1053	        stageTop: Float,
  1054	        stageScale: Float,
  1055	        progress: Float,
  1056	        alpha: Float,
  1057	        formFocusMotion: Float
  1058	    ) {
  1059	        val logoEntry = bouncySegmentMs(progress, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
  1060	        val finalProgress = easedSegmentMs(progress, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
  1061	        val focusShiftY = mockLogoFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  1062	        val base = offsetBounds(Bounds(222f, 150f, 750f, 535f), focusShiftY)
  1063	        val final = offsetBounds(Bounds(297f, LOGO_FINAL_TOP, 600f, LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT), focusShiftY)
  1064	
  1065	        if (logoEntry >= 0.999f) {
  1066	            drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoCombined, lerpBounds(base, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1067	            return
  1068	        }
  1069	
  1070	        val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(447f, 1037f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
  1071	        val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(453f, -417f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
  1072	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoSimple, lerpBounds(simpleEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1073	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoEyes, lerpBounds(eyesEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1074	    }
  1075	
  1076	    private fun drawIntroBackground(canvas: Canvas, solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float) {
  1077	        val standardSignInBackground = standardSignInBackgroundForSize()
  1078	        if (gradientProgress <= 0.001f) {
  1079	            canvas.drawColor(lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress))
  1080	            return
  1081	        }
  1082	
  1083	        val solid = lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress)
  1084	        backgroundPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  1085	            0f,
  1086	            0f,
  1087	            0f,
  1088	            height.toFloat(),
  1089	            intArrayOf(
  1090	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_TOP, gradientProgress),
  1091	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_CENTER, gradientProgress),
  1092	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_BOTTOM, gradientProgress)
  1093	            ),
  1094	            null,
  1095	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  1096	        )
  1097	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1098	        backgroundPaint.shader = null
  1099	
  1100	        if (standardSignInBackground != null) {
  1101	            drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas, gradientProgress)
  1102	            drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas, standardSignInBackground, gradientProgress)
  1103	        }
  1104	    }
  1105	
  1106	    private fun drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas: Canvas, alpha: Float) {
  1107	        backgroundPaint.color = COLOR_FRAME_1
  1108	        backgroundPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1109	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1110	        backgroundPaint.alpha = 255
  1111	    }
  1112	
  1113	    private fun drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas: Canvas, bitmap: Bitmap, alpha: Float) {
  1114	        tempRect.set(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat())
  1115	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * STANDARD_BACKGROUND_SETTLED_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1116	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1117	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1118	    }
  1119	
  1120	    private fun drawFigmaBitmap(
  1121	        canvas: Canvas,
  1122	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  1123	        bounds: Bounds,
  1124	        stageLeft: Float,
  1125	        stageTop: Float,
  1126	        stageScale: Float,
  1127	        alpha: Float
  1128	    ) {
  1129	        tempRect.set(
  1130	            stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale,
  1131	            stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale,
  1132	            stageLeft + (bounds.x + bounds.w) * stageScale,
  1133	            stageTop + (bounds.y + bounds.h) * stageScale
  1134	        )
  1135	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1136	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1137	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1138	    }
  1139	
  1140	    private fun drawAccountPrompt(
  1141	        canvas: Canvas,
  1142	        stageLeft: Float,
  1143	        stageTop: Float,
  1144	        stageScale: Float,
  1145	        yOffset: Float,
  1146	        alpha: Float
  1147	    ) {
  1148	        val isLandscape = width > height
  1149	        val textSize = signInAccountPromptTextSize() * stageScale
  1150	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1151	        accountPromptPaint.textSize = textSize
  1152	        formTitlePaint.textSize = textSize
  1153	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1154	        formTitlePaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1155	
  1156	        val accountPromptShiftY = if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  1157	        val centerY = stageTop +
  1158	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + accountPromptShiftY + yOffset) * stageScale
  1159	        val secondaryMetrics = accountPromptPaint.fontMetrics
  1160	        val primaryMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1161	        val secondaryBaselineY = centerY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1162	        val primaryBaselineY = centerY - (primaryMetrics.ascent + primaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1163	        if (
  1164	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  1165	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
  1166	        ) {
  1167	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1168	            canvas.drawText(
  1169	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT,
  1170	                stageLeft + accountPromptCreateCenterX() * stageScale,
  1171	                secondaryBaselineY,
  1172	                accountPromptPaint
  1173	            )
  1174	        }
  1175	        if (
  1176	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn &&
  1177	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn
  1178	        ) {
  1179	            formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1180	            canvas.drawText(
  1181	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1182	                stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale,
  1183	                primaryBaselineY,
  1184	                formTitlePaint
  1185	            )
  1186	        }
  1187	        if (
  1188	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  1189	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
  1190	        ) {
  1191	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1192	            canvas.drawText(
  1193	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT,
  1194	                stageLeft + accountPromptForgotCenterX() * stageScale,
  1195	                secondaryBaselineY,
  1196	                accountPromptPaint
  1197	            )
  1198	        }
  1199	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = 255
  1200	        accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1201	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1202	    }
  1203	
  1204	    private fun drawMockSignInForm(
  1205	        canvas: Canvas,
  1206	        stageLeft: Float,
  1207	        stageTop: Float,
  1208	        stageScale: Float,
  1209	        progress: Float,
  1210	        formFocusMotion: Float,
  1211	        updateLabelAnimation: Boolean = true
  1212	    ) {
  1213	        if (updateLabelAnimation) {
  1214	            updateMockFormLabelAnimation()
  1215	        }
  1216	
  1217	        val alpha = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1218	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1219	            drawMockCreateAccountForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1220	            return
  1221	        }
  1222	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  1223	            drawMockForgotPasswordForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1224	            return
  1225	        }
  1226	
  1227	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha)
  1228	        val fieldFillAlpha = (alpha * 26f).roundToInt()
  1229	        val fieldStrokeAlpha = (alpha * 185f).roundToInt()
  1230	        val fieldX = mockFormX()
  1231	        val usernameY = MOCK_FORM_Y + yOffset
  1232	        val passwordY = usernameY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_GAP
  1233	
  1234	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fieldFillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1235	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(fieldStrokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1236	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1237	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
  1238	
  1239	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  1240	        val rememberMeY = passwordY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TOP_GAP + focusShiftY
  1241	        drawMockSignInTitle(canvas, usernameY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1242	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Username, fieldX, usernameY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1243	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Password, fieldX, passwordY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1244	        drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  1245	            canvas,
  1246	            fieldX,
  1247	            rememberMeY,
  1248	            stageLeft,
  1249	            stageTop,
  1250	            stageScale,
  1251	            alpha
  1252	        )
  1253	        drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1254	            canvas,
  1255	            stageLeft,
  1256	            stageTop,
  1257	            stageScale,
  1258	            alpha,
  1259	            yOffset,
  1260	            focusShiftY
  1261	        )
  1262	
  1263	        formLabelPaint.alpha = 255
  1264	        formInputPaint.alpha = 255
  1265	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1266	        formCaretPaint.alpha = 255
  1267	    }

  1448	
  1449	    private fun drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1450	        canvas: Canvas,
  1451	        stageLeft: Float,
  1452	        stageTop: Float,
  1453	        stageScale: Float,
  1454	        alpha: Float,
  1455	        yOffset: Float,
  1456	        focusShiftY: Float
  1457	    ) {
  1458	        val dimProgress = mockInputDimProgress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1459	        if (dimProgress <= 0.01f) return
  1460	
  1461	        inputDimPaint.alpha = (dimProgress * MOCK_INPUT_DIM_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1462	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), inputDimPaint)
  1463	        inputDimPaint.alpha = 255
  1464	
  1465	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 26f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1466	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 185f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1467	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1468	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
  1469	
  1470	        val activeDropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: mockInputDimActiveDropdown
  1471	        if (activeDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1472	            drawMockDropdownControl(
  1473	                canvas,
  1474	                activeDropdown,
  1475	                mockDropdownX(activeDropdown),
  1476	                mockDropdownY(activeDropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1477	                stageLeft,
  1478	                stageTop,
  1479	                stageScale,
  1480	                alpha,
  1481	                mockDropdownWidth(activeDropdown)
  1482	            )
  1483	            if (expandedMockDropdown == activeDropdown) {
  1484	                drawMockDropdownOptions(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1485	            }
  1486	            return
  1487	        }
  1488	
  1489	        val activeField = focusedMockField ?: mockInputDimActiveField ?: return
  1490	        drawMockSignInField(
  1491	            canvas,
  1492	            activeField,
  1493	            mockActiveFieldX(activeField),
  1494	            mockActiveFieldY(activeField, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1495	            stageLeft,
  1496	            stageTop,
  1497	            stageScale,
  1498	            alpha,
  1499	            mockActiveFieldWidth(activeField)
  1500	        )
  1501	    }

  1503	    private fun drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
  1504	        canvas: Canvas,
  1505	        flow: MockAccountFlow,
  1506	        stageLeft: Float,
  1507	        stageTop: Float,
  1508	        stageScale: Float,
  1509	        progress: Float,
  1510	        formFocusMotion: Float,
  1511	        updateLabelAnimation: Boolean
  1512	    ) {
  1513	        val currentFlow = activeMockFlow
  1514	        activeMockFlow = flow
  1515	        try {
  1516	            drawMockSignInForm(
  1517	                canvas,
  1518	                stageLeft,
  1519	                stageTop,
  1520	                stageScale,
  1521	                progress,
  1522	                formFocusMotion,
  1523	                updateLabelAnimation
  1524	            )
  1525	        } finally {
  1526	            activeMockFlow = currentFlow
  1527	        }
  1528	    }

  1530	    private fun drawMockSignInTitle(
  1531	        canvas: Canvas,
  1532	        fieldTopY: Float,
  1533	        stageLeft: Float,
  1534	        stageTop: Float,
  1535	        stageScale: Float,
  1536	        alpha: Float
  1537	    ) {
  1538	        val motion = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1539	        val sourceTextSize = signInAccountPromptTextSize() * stageScale
  1540	        val targetTextSize = MOCK_FORM_TITLE_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1541	        val sourceCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  1542	        val targetCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  1543	        val sourceCenterY = stageTop +
  1544	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY()) * stageScale
  1545	        formTitlePaint.textSize = sourceTextSize
  1546	        val sourceMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1547	        val sourceBaselineY = sourceCenterY - (sourceMetrics.ascent + sourceMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1548	        val targetBaselineY = stageTop + (fieldTopY - MOCK_FORM_TITLE_BASELINE_GAP) * stageScale
  1549	
  1550	        formTitlePaint.textSize = lerpFloat(sourceTextSize, targetTextSize, motion)
  1551	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1552	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1553	        canvas.drawText(
  1554	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1555	            lerpFloat(sourceCenterX, targetCenterX, motion),
  1556	            lerpFloat(sourceBaselineY, targetBaselineY, motion),
  1557	            formTitlePaint
  1558	        )
  1559	    }

  1622	
  1623	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButton(
  1624	        canvas: Canvas,
  1625	        x: Float,
  1626	        y: Float,
  1627	        stageLeft: Float,
  1628	        stageTop: Float,
  1629	        stageScale: Float,
  1630	        alpha: Float,
  1631	        buttonWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  1632	    ) {
  1633	        tempRect.set(
  1634	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  1635	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  1636	            stageLeft + (x + buttonWidth) * stageScale,
  1637	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  1638	        )
  1639	        val radius = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_RADIUS * stageScale
  1640	        val submissionState = forgotPasswordSubmissionState
  1641	        val enabled = mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  1642	        val pressed = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit && enabled
  1643	        val fillAlpha = when {
  1644	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 86f
  1645	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 72f
  1646	            pressed -> 92f
  1647	            enabled -> 68f
  1648	            else -> 18f
  1649	        }
  1650	        val strokeAlpha = when {
  1651	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 245f
  1652	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 228f
  1653	            pressed -> 238f
  1654	            enabled -> 210f
  1655	            else -> 118f
  1656	        }
  1657	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  1658	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * fillAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1659	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * strokeAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1660	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1661	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  1662	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  1663	        if (submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading) {
  1664	            drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(canvas, tempRect, radius, alpha)
  1665	        }
  1666	
  1667	        formTitlePaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1668	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1669	        val metrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1670	        val baselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  1671	        val maxTextWidth = buttonWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  1672	        val showCheck = submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  1673	        val checkSize = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_SIZE * stageScale else 0f
  1674	        val checkGap = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_GAP * stageScale else 0f
  1675	        val textMaxWidth = (maxTextWidth - checkSize - checkGap).coerceAtLeast(maxTextWidth * 0.5f)
  1676	        val buttonText = trailingFittingText(
  1677	            mockForgotPasswordButtonText(),
  1678	            if (showCheck) textMaxWidth else maxTextWidth,
  1679	            formTitlePaint
  1680	        )
  1681	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * when {
  1682	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 226f
  1683	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 255f
  1684	            enabled -> 255f
  1685	            else -> 138f
  1686	        }).roundToInt()
  1687	        formTitlePaint.alpha = textAlpha
  1688	        val textX = if (showCheck) {
  1689	            val textWidth = formTitlePaint.measureText(buttonText)
  1690	            val groupWidth = checkSize + checkGap + textWidth
  1691	            val groupLeft = tempRect.centerX() - groupWidth / 2f
  1692	            val checkLeft = groupLeft
  1693	            val checkCenterY = tempRect.centerY()
  1694	            backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  1695	            backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 2.1f * stageScale
  1696	            canvas.drawLine(
  1697	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.18f,
  1698	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.03f,
  1699	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  1700	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  1701	                backChevronPaint
  1702	            )
  1703	            canvas.drawLine(
  1704	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  1705	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  1706	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.84f,
  1707	                checkCenterY - checkSize * 0.28f,
  1708	                backChevronPaint
  1709	            )
  1710	            groupLeft + checkSize + checkGap + textWidth / 2f
  1711	        } else {
  1712	            tempRect.centerX()
  1713	        }
  1714	        canvas.drawText(
  1715	            buttonText,
  1716	            textX,
  1717	            baselineY,
  1718	            formTitlePaint
  1719	        )
  1720	    }
  1721	
  1722	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(
  1723	        canvas: Canvas,
  1724	        buttonRect: RectF,
  1725	        radius: Float,
  1726	        alpha: Float
  1727	    ) {
  1728	        val progress = mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress()
  1729	        val sweepWidth = buttonRect.width() * 0.44f
  1730	        val sweepCenterX = buttonRect.left + buttonRect.width() * (progress * 1.35f - 0.18f)
  1731	        val sweepLeft = sweepCenterX - sweepWidth / 2f
  1732	        val sweepRight = sweepCenterX + sweepWidth / 2f
  1733	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  1734	            sweepLeft,
  1735	            buttonRect.top,
  1736	            sweepRight,
  1737	            buttonRect.top,
  1738	            intArrayOf(
  1739	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255),
  1740	                Color.argb((alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 54f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255),
  1741	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255)
  1742	            ),
  1743	            floatArrayOf(0f, 0.5f, 1f),
  1744	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  1745	        )
  1746	        canvas.save()
  1747	        tempPath.reset()
  1748	        tempPath.addRoundRect(buttonRect, radius, radius, Path.Direction.CW)
  1749	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  1750	        canvas.drawRect(sweepLeft, buttonRect.top, sweepRight, buttonRect.bottom, formFieldFillPaint)
  1751	        canvas.restore()
  1752	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  1753	    }

  1804	            layout.passwordBoxX,

  1812	    private fun drawMockForgotRecoveryOption(
  1813	        canvas: Canvas,
  1814	        target: MockForgotRecoveryTarget,
  1815	        left: Float,
  1816	        top: Float,
  1817	        boxWidth: Float,
  1818	        stageScale: Float,
  1819	        alpha: Float
  1820	    ) {
  1821	        val right = left + boxWidth
  1822	        val bottom = top + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_HEIGHT * stageScale
  1823	        tempRect.set(left, top, right, bottom)
  1824	        val selected = forgotRecoveryTarget == target
  1825	        val pressed = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == target
  1826	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * when {
  1827	            pressed -> 82f
  1828	            selected -> 64f
  1829	            else -> 14f
  1830	        }).roundToInt()
  1831	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * when {
  1832	            pressed -> 245f
  1833	            selected -> 235f
  1834	            else -> 142f
  1835	        }).roundToInt()
  1836	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  1837	
  1838	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1839	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(strokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1840	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1841	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  1842	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  1843	
  1844	        formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_OPTION_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1845	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (selected || pressed) 255f else 178f).roundToInt()
  1846	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1847	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  1848	        canvas.drawText(
  1849	            target.displayText,
  1850	            tempRect.centerX(),
  1851	            tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  1852	            formInputPaint
  1853	        )
  1854	    }

  1856	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordBodyParagraph(
  1857	        canvas: Canvas,
  1858	        text: String,
  1859	        centerX: Float,
  1860	        topY: Float,
  1861	        maxWidth: Float,
  1862	        stageLeft: Float,
  1863	        stageTop: Float,
  1864	        stageScale: Float,
  1865	        alpha: Float
  1866	    ): Float {
  1867	        val paragraphs = text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n"))
  1868	        val leadBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  1869	            canvas,
  1870	            paragraphs.firstOrNull().orEmpty(),
  1871	            centerX,
  1872	            topY,
  1873	            maxWidth,
  1874	            stageLeft,
  1875	            stageTop,
  1876	            stageScale,
  1877	            alpha,
  1878	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_TEXT_SIZE,
  1879	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_LINE_HEIGHT
  1880	        )
  1881	        val middleText = paragraphs.getOrNull(1) ?: return leadBottomY
  1882	        val middleBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  1883	            canvas,
  1884	            middleText,
  1885	            centerX,
  1886	            leadBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  1887	            maxWidth,
  1888	            stageLeft,
  1889	            stageTop,
  1890	            stageScale,
  1891	            alpha,
  1892	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  1893	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  1894	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  1895	        )
  1896	        val bottomText = paragraphs.drop(2).joinToString("\n\n")
  1897	        if (bottomText.isEmpty()) return middleBottomY
  1898	        return drawMockFormParagraph(
  1899	            canvas,
  1900	            bottomText,
  1901	            centerX,
  1902	            middleBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  1903	            maxWidth,
  1904	            stageLeft,
  1905	            stageTop,
  1906	            stageScale,
  1907	            alpha,
  1908	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  1909	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  1910	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  1911	        )
  1912	    }
  1913	
  1914	    private fun drawMockFormParagraph(
  1915	        canvas: Canvas,
  1916	        text: String,
  1917	        centerX: Float,
  1918	        topY: Float,
  1919	        maxWidth: Float,
  1920	        stageLeft: Float,
  1921	        stageTop: Float,
  1922	        stageScale: Float,
  1923	        alpha: Float,
  1924	        textSize: Float,
  1925	        lineHeight: Float,
  1926	        textAlign: Paint.Align = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1927	    ): Float {
  1928	        formInputPaint.textSize = textSize * stageScale
  1929	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 218f).roundToInt()
  1930	        formInputPaint.textAlign = textAlign
  1931	
  1932	        val lines = wrappedTextLines(text, maxWidth * stageScale, formInputPaint)
  1933	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  1934	        val x = when (textAlign) {
  1935	            Paint.Align.LEFT -> stageLeft + (centerX - maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  1936	            Paint.Align.RIGHT -> stageLeft + (centerX + maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  1937	            Paint.Align.CENTER -> stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  1938	        }
  1939	        var baselineY = stageTop + topY * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  1940	        lines.forEach { line ->
  1941	            canvas.drawText(line, x, baselineY, formInputPaint)
  1942	            baselineY += lineHeight * stageScale
  1943	        }
  1944	        return topY + lines.size * lineHeight
  1945	    }

  1947	    private fun drawMockCreateAccountAvatarText(
  1948	        canvas: Canvas,
  1949	        columnX: Float,
  1950	        firstFieldY: Float,
  1951	        stageLeft: Float,
  1952	        stageTop: Float,
  1953	        stageScale: Float,
  1954	        alpha: Float
  1955	    ) {
  1956	        val avatarScale = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE
  1957	        val scaledAvatarSize = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * avatarScale
  1958	        val labelBaselineFromTop = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LABEL_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * avatarScale
  1959	        val avatarTopGap = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_TOP_GAP * avatarScale
  1960	        val centerX = stageLeft + (columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f) * stageScale
  1961	        val baselineY = stageTop + (firstFieldY + labelBaselineFromTop) * stageScale
  1962	        formLabelPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * avatarScale * stageScale
  1963	        formLabelPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1964	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1965	        canvas.drawText(
  1966	            MOCK_FORM_AVATAR_TEXT,
  1967	            centerX,
  1968	            baselineY,
  1969	            formLabelPaint
  1970	        )
  1971	
  1972	        val avatarLeftX = columnX + (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH - scaledAvatarSize) / 2f
  1973	        val avatarTopY = firstFieldY + labelBaselineFromTop + avatarTopGap
  1974	        val avatarBounds = Bounds(
  1975	            avatarLeftX,
  1976	            avatarTopY,
  1977	            scaledAvatarSize,
  1978	            scaledAvatarSize
  1979	        )
  1980	        val carouselProgress = mockCreateAvatarCarouselProgress()
  1981	        val slideDirection = mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  1982	        val slideDistance = mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(scaledAvatarSize)
  1983	        val dragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
  1984	        val dragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(slideDistance, stageScale)
  1985	
  1986	        drawMockCreateAvatarBubbleShell(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1987	
  1988	        canvas.save()
  1989	        clipMockCreateAvatarBubbleInterior(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale)
  1990	        if (
  1991	            activeMockCreateAvatarDrag &&
  1992	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  1993	            dragDirection != null &&
  1994	            !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
  1995	        ) {
  1996	            val dragSlideDirection = dragDirection.stageDirection
  1997	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  1998	                canvas,
  1999	                mockCreateAvatarIndex,
  2000	                avatarBounds,
  2001	                -dragSlideDirection * dragProgress * slideDistance,
  2002	                stageLeft,
  2003	                stageTop,
  2004	                stageScale,
  2005	                alpha
  2006	            )
  2007	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2008	                canvas,
  2009	                mockCreateAvatarIndexForDirection(dragDirection),
  2010	                avatarBounds,
  2011	                dragSlideDirection * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance,
  2012	                stageLeft,
  2013	                stageTop,
  2014	                stageScale,
  2015	                alpha
  2016	            )
  2017	        } else if (isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()) {
  2018	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2019	                canvas,
  2020	                mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex,
  2021	                avatarBounds,
  2022	                -slideDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance,
  2023	                stageLeft,
  2024	                stageTop,
  2025	                stageScale,
  2026	                alpha
  2027	            )
  2028	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2029	                canvas,
  2030	                mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex,
  2031	                avatarBounds,
  2032	                slideDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance,
  2033	                stageLeft,
  2034	                stageTop,
  2035	                stageScale,
  2036	                alpha
  2037	            )
  2038	        } else {
  2039	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2040	                canvas,
  2041	                mockCreateAvatarIndex,
  2042	                avatarBounds,
  2043	                0f,
  2044	                stageLeft,
  2045	                stageTop,
  2046	                stageScale,
  2047	                alpha
  2048	            )
  2049	        }
  2050	        canvas.restore()
  2051	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2052	            canvas,
  2053	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous,
  2054	            avatarBounds,
  2055	            stageLeft,
  2056	            stageTop,
  2057	            stageScale,
  2058	            alpha
  2059	        )
  2060	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2061	            canvas,
  2062	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next,
  2063	            avatarBounds,
  2064	            stageLeft,
  2065	            stageTop,
  2066	            stageScale,
  2067	            alpha
  2068	        )
  2069	    }

  2111	    private fun clipMockCreateAvatarBubbleInterior(
  2112	        canvas: Canvas,
  2113	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2114	        stageLeft: Float,
  2115	        stageTop: Float,
  2116	        stageScale: Float
  2117	    ) {
  2118	        tempPath.reset()
  2119	        tempPath.addCircle(
  2120	            stageLeft + (avatarBounds.x + avatarBounds.w / 2f) * stageScale,
  2121	            stageTop + (avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h / 2f) * stageScale,
  2122	            mockCreateAvatarBubbleInnerRadius(avatarBounds, stageScale),
  2123	            Path.Direction.CW
  2124	        )
  2125	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  2126	    }

  2128	    private fun mockCreateAvatarBubbleInnerRadius(avatarBounds: Bounds, stageScale: Float): Float {
  2129	        return (avatarBounds.w / 2f - avatarBounds.w * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO) * stageScale
  2130	    }

  2132	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2133	        canvas: Canvas,
  2134	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  2135	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2136	        stageLeft: Float,
  2137	        stageTop: Float,
  2138	        stageScale: Float,
  2139	        alpha: Float
  2140	    ) {
  2141	        val centerX = stageLeft + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * stageScale
  2142	        val centerY = stageTop + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds) * stageScale
  2143	        val halfWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2144	        val halfHeight = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * stageScale / 2f
  2145	        val pressedAlpha = if (pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == direction) 0.62f else 0.9f
  2146	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * pressedAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  2147	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2148	
  2149	        if (direction == MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous) {
  2150	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX - halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  2151	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY, centerX + halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  2152	        } else {
  2153	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX + halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  2154	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY, centerX - halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  2155	        }
  2156	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2157	    }

  2159	    private fun drawMockDropdownControl(
  2160	        canvas: Canvas,
  2161	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  2162	        x: Float,
  2163	        y: Float,
  2164	        stageLeft: Float,
  2165	        stageTop: Float,
  2166	        stageScale: Float,
  2167	        alpha: Float,
  2168	        dropdownWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2169	    ) {
  2170	        tempRect.set(
  2171	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  2172	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  2173	            stageLeft + (x + dropdownWidth) * stageScale,
  2174	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  2175	        )
  2176	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2177	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2178	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2179	
  2180	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2181	        formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  2182	        formInputPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2183	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2184	        val textInset = mockDropdownTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2185	        val chevronInset = mockDropdownChevronInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2186	        val displayText = mockDropdownDisplayText(dropdown)
  2187	        val membershipIcon = membershipTierArtworkIconForOption(displayText)
  2188	            ?.takeIf { dropdown == MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier }
  2189	        val iconLaneWidth = if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2190	            (MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_CONTROL_ICON_SIZE + MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_ICON_TEXT_GAP) * stageScale
  2191	        } else {
  2192	            0f
  2193	        }
  2194	        val maxTextWidth = (dropdownWidth * stageScale - textInset - iconLaneWidth - chevronInset * 2f)
  2195	            .coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2196	        if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2197	            drawMembershipTierIcon(
  2198	                canvas = canvas,
  2199	                bitmap = membershipIcon,
  2200	                left = tempRect.left + textInset,
  2201	                centerY = tempRect.centerY(),
  2202	                iconSize = MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_CONTROL_ICON_SIZE * stageScale,
  2203	                alpha = textAlpha
  2204	            )
  2205	        }
  2206	        canvas.drawText(
  2207	            trailingFittingText(displayText, maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  2208	            tempRect.left + textInset + iconLaneWidth,
  2209	            tempRect.centerY() - (formInputPaint.fontMetrics.ascent + formInputPaint.fontMetrics.descent) / 2f,
  2210	            formInputPaint
  2211	        )
  2212	
  2213	        val centerX = tempRect.right - chevronInset
  2214	        val centerY = tempRect.centerY() + 2f * stageScale
  2215	        val halfSize = mockDropdownChevronSize(dropdown) * stageScale / 2f
  2216	        backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2217	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 1.65f * stageScale
  2218	        canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  2219	        canvas.drawLine(centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, centerX + halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  2220	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2221	    }

  2223	    private fun drawMockDropdownOptions(
  2224	        canvas: Canvas,
  2225	        stageLeft: Float,
  2226	        stageTop: Float,
  2227	        stageScale: Float,
  2228	        alpha: Float
  2229	    ) {
  2230	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return
  2231	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return
  2232	
  2233	        val x = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  2234	        val y = mockDropdownOptionsY(
  2235	            dropdown,
  2236	            MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha),
  2237	            mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  2238	        )
  2239	        val left = stageLeft + x * stageScale
  2240	        val top = stageTop + y * stageScale
  2241	        val right = stageLeft + (x + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stageScale
  2242	        val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stageScale
  2243	        val optionAlpha = 255
  2244	        val optionTextInset = mockDropdownOptionTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2245	        val scrollbarReservedWidth = if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  2246	            (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stageScale
  2247	        } else {
  2248	            0f
  2249	        }
  2250	
  2251	        val visibleOptions = mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown)
  2252	        visibleOptions.forEachIndexed { index, option ->
  2253	            val optionTop = top + index * optionHeight
  2254	            val optionBottom = optionTop + optionHeight
  2255	            val fillColor = if (option == mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown)) {
  2256	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 76, 12, 94)
  2257	            } else {
  2258	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 14, 2, 18)
  2259	            }
  2260	            tempRect.set(left, optionTop, right, optionBottom)
  2261	            formFieldFillPaint.color = fillColor
  2262	            formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(optionAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2263	            formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = 1.2f * stageScale
  2264	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldFillPaint)
  2265	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2266	
  2267	            formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownOptionTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  2268	            formInputPaint.alpha = optionAlpha
  2269	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2270	            val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2271	            val membershipIcon = membershipTierArtworkIconForOption(option)
  2272	                ?.takeIf { dropdown == MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier }
  2273	            val iconLaneWidth = if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2274	                (MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_OPTION_ICON_SIZE + MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_ICON_TEXT_GAP) * stageScale
  2275	            } else {
  2276	                0f
  2277	            }
  2278	            if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2279	                drawMembershipTierIcon(
  2280	                    canvas = canvas,
  2281	                    bitmap = membershipIcon,
  2282	                    left = left + optionTextInset,
  2283	                    centerY = (optionTop + optionBottom) / 2f,
  2284	                    iconSize = MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_OPTION_ICON_SIZE * stageScale,
  2285	                    alpha = optionAlpha
  2286	                )
  2287	            }
  2288	            val maxTextWidth = (right - left - optionTextInset - iconLaneWidth - scrollbarReservedWidth)
  2289	                .coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2290	            canvas.drawText(
  2291	                trailingFittingText(option, maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  2292	                left + optionTextInset + iconLaneWidth,
  2293	                (optionTop + optionBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  2294	                formInputPaint
  2295	            )
  2296	        }
  2297	
  2298	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  2299	            drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  2300	                canvas,
  2301	                dropdown,
  2302	                top,
  2303	                right,
  2304	                optionHeight * visibleOptions.size,
  2305	                stageScale,
  2306	                optionAlpha
  2307	            )
  2308	        }
  2309	    }

  2311	    private fun drawMembershipTierIcon(
  2312	        canvas: Canvas,
  2313	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  2314	        left: Float,
  2315	        centerY: Float,
  2316	        iconSize: Float,
  2317	        alpha: Int
  2318	    ) {
  2319	        val bitmapAspect = bitmap.width.toFloat() / bitmap.height.toFloat()
  2320	        val width: Float
  2321	        val height: Float
  2322	        if (bitmapAspect >= 1f) {
  2323	            width = iconSize
  2324	            height = iconSize / bitmapAspect
  2325	        } else {
  2326	            width = iconSize * bitmapAspect
  2327	            height = iconSize
  2328	        }
  2329	        val top = centerY - height / 2f
  2330	        tempIconRect.set(left, top, left + width, top + height)
  2331	        imagePaint.alpha = alpha.coerceIn(0, 255)
  2332	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempIconRect, imagePaint)
  2333	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  2334	    }

  2336	    private fun drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  2337	        canvas: Canvas,
  2338	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  2339	        top: Float,
  2340	        right: Float,
  2341	        dropdownHeight: Float,
  2342	        stageScale: Float,
  2343	        alpha: Int
  2344	    ) {
  2345	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  2346	        val visibleCount = mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  2347	        val maxOffset = mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown)
  2348	        if (options.isEmpty() || maxOffset <= 0) return
  2349	
  2350	        val trackRight = right - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * stageScale
  2351	        val trackLeft = trackRight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale
  2352	        val trackTop = top + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  2353	        val trackBottom = top + dropdownHeight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  2354	        val trackHeight = (trackBottom - trackTop).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2355	        val thumbHeight = maxOf(14f * stageScale, trackHeight * visibleCount / options.size)
  2356	            .coerceAtMost(trackHeight)
  2357	        val thumbProgress = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown) / maxOffset.toFloat()
  2358	        val thumbTop = trackTop + (trackHeight - thumbHeight) * thumbProgress
  2359	        val radius = MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2360	
  2361	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, trackTop, trackRight, trackBottom)
  2362	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.28f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2363	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2364	
  2365	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, thumbTop, trackRight, thumbTop + thumbHeight)
  2366	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.78f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2367	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2368	    }
