  2370	    private fun drawMockSignInField(
  2371	        canvas: Canvas,
  2372	        field: MockSignInField,
  2373	        x: Float,
  2374	        y: Float,
  2375	        stageLeft: Float,
  2376	        stageTop: Float,
  2377	        stageScale: Float,
  2378	        alpha: Float,
  2379	        fieldWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2380	    ) {
  2381	        tempRect.set(
  2382	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  2383	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  2384	            stageLeft + (x + fieldWidth) * stageScale,
  2385	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  2386	        )
  2387	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2388	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2389	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2390	
  2391	        val label = mockSignInFieldLabel(field)
  2392	        val labelProgress = mockSignInFieldLabelProgress(field)
  2393	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2394	        formLabelPaint.textSize = lerpFloat(
  2395	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE,
  2396	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * MOCK_FORM_FLOATING_LABEL_SCALE,
  2397	            labelProgress
  2398	        ) * stageScale
  2399	        formLabelPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2400	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2401	        val metrics = formLabelPaint.fontMetrics
  2402	        val placeholderBaselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2403	        val floatingBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_TOP_INSET_Y * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  2404	        val labelX = lerpFloat(
  2405	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale,
  2406	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_INSET_X * stageScale,
  2407	            labelProgress
  2408	        )
  2409	        val labelBaselineY = lerpFloat(placeholderBaselineY, floatingBaselineY, labelProgress)
  2410	        canvas.drawText(
  2411	            label,
  2412	            labelX,
  2413	            labelBaselineY,
  2414	            formLabelPaint
  2415	        )
  2416	
  2417	        val fieldText = mockSignInFieldDisplayText(field)
  2418	        val shouldDrawInput = fieldText.isNotEmpty() || focusedMockField == field
  2419	        if (shouldDrawInput) {
  2420	            val inputAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * labelProgress * 255f).roundToInt()
  2421	            formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2422	            formInputPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  2423	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2424	            formCaretPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  2425	
  2426	            val inputX = tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale
  2427	            val inputBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_INPUT_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * stageScale
  2428	            val maxInputWidth = fieldWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  2429	            val visibleText = trailingFittingText(fieldText, maxInputWidth, formInputPaint)
  2430	            canvas.drawText(visibleText, inputX, inputBaselineY, formInputPaint)
  2431	
  2432	            if (focusedMockField == field) {
  2433	                val caretX = inputX + formInputPaint.measureText(visibleText) + 2f * stageScale
  2434	                val inputMetrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2435	                canvas.drawLine(
  2436	                    caretX,
  2437	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.ascent,
  2438	                    caretX,
  2439	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.descent,
  2440	                    formCaretPaint
  2441	                )
  2442	            }
  2443	        }
  2444	    }

  2446	    private fun drawRememberMeCheckbox(

  2489	        }
  2490

  2492	        rememberMeTextPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  2493	        rememberMeTextPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2494	        val metrics = rememberMeTextPaint.fontMetrics
  2495	        val baselineY = (boxTop + boxBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2496	        canvas.drawText(
  2497	            MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT,

  2500	            rememberMeTextPaint
  2501	        )

  2505	    private fun drawBackChevron(
  2506	        canvas: Canvas,

  2508	        topY: Float,

  2510	        stageTop: Float,

  2515	        val center = stageLeft + centerX * stageScale

  2527	    private fun decode(resId: Int): Bitmap {
  2528	        return BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, resId, bitmapOptions)
  2529	    }
  2530	
  2531	    private fun createAvatarCharacterBitmap(
  2532	        source: Bitmap,
  2533	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean = false
  2534	    ): Bitmap {
  2535	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  2536	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  2537	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  2538	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2539	
  2540	        val mask = createAvatarCharacterMask(pixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, includeDarkSaturatedPixels)
  2541	        val outputPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  2542	            if (mask[index]) pixels[index] else Color.TRANSPARENT
  2543	        }
  2544	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  2545	            setPixels(outputPixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2546	        }
  2547	    }
  2548	
  2549	    private fun createAvatarBubbleShellBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  2550	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  2551	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  2552	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  2553	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2554	
  2555	        val centerX = (bitmapWidth - 1f) / 2f
  2556	        val centerY = (bitmapHeight - 1f) / 2f
  2557	        val innerRadius = min(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight) * (0.5f - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO)
  2558	        val innerRadiusSquared = innerRadius * innerRadius
  2559	
  2560	        val outputPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  2561	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  2562	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  2563	            val dx = x - centerX
  2564	            val dy = y - centerY
  2565	            if (dx * dx + dy * dy < innerRadiusSquared) {
  2566	                Color.TRANSPARENT
  2567	            } else {
  2568	                pixels[index]
  2569	            }
  2570	        }
  2571	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  2572	            setPixels(outputPixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2573	        }
  2574	    }
  2575	
  2576	    private fun createAvatarCharacterMask(
  2577	        pixels: IntArray,
  2578	        bitmapWidth: Int,
  2579	        bitmapHeight: Int,
  2580	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean
  2581	    ): BooleanArray {
  2582	        val coloredPixels = BooleanArray(pixels.size)
  2583	        pixels.forEachIndexed { index, pixel ->
  2584	            coloredPixels[index] = isColorfulAvatarPixel(pixel, includeDarkSaturatedPixels)
  2585	        }
  2586	
  2587	        val mask = BooleanArray(pixels.size)
  2588	        val outlineRadius = 7
  2589	        for (y in 0 until bitmapHeight) {
  2590	            for (x in 0 until bitmapWidth) {
  2591	                val index = y * bitmapWidth + x
  2592	                if (!coloredPixels[index]) continue
  2593	
  2594	                mask[index] = true
  2595	                for (dy in -outlineRadius..outlineRadius) {
  2596	                    val sampleY = y + dy
  2597	                    if (sampleY !in 0 until bitmapHeight) continue
  2598	                    for (dx in -outlineRadius..outlineRadius) {
  2599	                        val sampleX = x + dx
  2600	                        if (sampleX !in 0 until bitmapWidth) continue
  2601	                        val sampleIndex = sampleY * bitmapWidth + sampleX
  2602	                        val samplePixel = pixels[sampleIndex]
  2603	                        val sampleAlpha = Color.alpha(samplePixel)
  2604	                        val sampleMax = maxOf(
  2605	                            Color.red(samplePixel),
  2606	                            Color.green(samplePixel),
  2607	                            Color.blue(samplePixel)
  2608	                        )
  2609	                        if (sampleAlpha > 16 && sampleMax <= 44) {
  2610	                            mask[sampleIndex] = true
  2611	                        }
  2612	                    }
  2613	                }
  2614	            }
  2615	        }
  2616	        return mask
  2617	    }
  2618	
  2619	    private fun isColorfulAvatarPixel(
  2620	        pixel: Int,
  2621	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean
  2622	    ): Boolean {
  2623	        val alpha = Color.alpha(pixel)
  2624	        val red = Color.red(pixel)
  2625	        val green = Color.green(pixel)
  2626	        val blue = Color.blue(pixel)
  2627	        val channelMax = maxOf(red, green, blue)
  2628	        val channelMin = minOf(red, green, blue)
  2629	        if (alpha <= 16) return false
  2630	
  2631	        val chroma = channelMax - channelMin
  2632	        return (channelMax > 48 && chroma > 22) ||
  2633	            (includeDarkSaturatedPixels && channelMax > 36 && chroma > 14)
  2634	    }
  2635	
  2636	    private fun standardSignInBackgroundForSize(): Bitmap? {
  2637	        return when {
  2638	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) &&
  2639	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) -> standardLandscapeBackground
  2640	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) &&
  2641	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) -> standardPortraitBackground
  2642	            else -> null
  2643	        }
  2644	    }
  2645	
  2646	    private fun isSizeCloseTo(actual: Int, expected: Int): Boolean {
  2647	        return abs(actual - expected) <= STANDARD_TABLET_SIZE_TOLERANCE
  2648	    }

  2650	    private fun isFinalFrameSettled(): Boolean {
  2651	        return bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis) >= 1f
  2652	    }
  2653	
  2654	    private fun settledAvatarHitProfile(x: Float, y: Float): CinerificProfile? {
  2655	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) return null
  2656	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return null
  2657	
  2658	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  2659	
  2660	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
  2661	        return FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.firstOrNull { target ->
  2662	            val bounds = target.bounds
  2663	            val centerX = stage.left + (bounds.x + bounds.w / 2f) * stage.scale
  2664	            val centerY = stage.top + (bounds.y + stackShiftY + bounds.h / 2f) * stage.scale
  2665	            val radius = min(bounds.w, bounds.h) * stage.scale / 2f
  2666	            val dx = x - centerX
  2667	            val dy = y - centerY
  2668	            dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius
  2669	        }?.profile
  2670	    }

  2672	    private fun settledCreateAccountPromptHit(x: Float, y: Float): Boolean {
  2673	        if (!accountPromptCanOpen(MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount)) return false
  2674	
  2675	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return false
  2676	        val centerX = stage.left + accountPromptCreateCenterX() * stage.scale
  2677	        val centerY = accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale) +
  2678	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY()) * stage.scale
  2679	        val halfWidth = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_HIT_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  2680	        val halfHeight = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_HIT_HEIGHT * stage.scale / 2f
  2681	        return x in (centerX - halfWidth)..(centerX + halfWidth) &&
  2682	            y in (centerY - halfHeight)..(centerY + halfHeight)
  2683	    }
  2684

  2687

  2689	        val centerX = stage.left + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stage.scale
  2690	        val centerY = accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale) +
  2691	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY()) * stage.scale
  2692	        val halfWidth = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_HIT_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  2693	        val halfHeight = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_HIT_HEIGHT * stage.scale / 2f
  2694	        return x in (centerX - halfWidth)..(centerX + halfWidth) &&
  2695	            y in (centerY - halfHeight)..(centerY + halfHeight)
  2696	    }

  2711	    private fun accountPromptCanOpen(flow: MockAccountFlow): Boolean {

  2837	    private fun updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  2838	        if (!activeMockCreateAvatarDrag) return false
  2839	
  2840	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  2841	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockTouchDownY
  2842	        if (
  2843	            !mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  2844	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > touchSlop &&
  2845	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > abs(dragDeltaY)

  2847	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = true
  2848	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  2849	        }
  2850	
  2851	        if (mockCreateAvatarDragMoved) {
  2852	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  2853	        }

  2855	    }

  2857	    private fun mockDropdownOptionHit(pointerX: Float, pointerY: Float): MockDropdownOption? {
  2858	        if (
  2859	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  2860	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount ||
  2861	            expandedMockDropdown == null
  2862	        ) {
  2863	            return null
  2864	        }
  2865	
  2866	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return null
  2867	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  2868	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  2869	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  2870	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  2871	        val dropdownX = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  2872	        val yStart = mockDropdownOptionsY(dropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  2873	        val left = stage.left + dropdownX * stage.scale
  2874	        val right = stage.left + (dropdownX + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stage.scale
  2875	        if (pointerX !in left..right) return null
  2876	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  2877	            val scrollbarLeft = right -
  2878	                (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stage.scale
  2879	            if (pointerX >= scrollbarLeft) return null
  2880	        }
  2881	
  2882	        mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown).forEachIndexed { optionIndex, option ->
  2883	            val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown)
  2884	            val top = stage.top + (yStart + optionIndex * optionHeight) * stage.scale
  2885	            val bottom = top + optionHeight * stage.scale
  2886	            if (pointerY in top..bottom) return MockDropdownOption(dropdown, option)
  2887	        }
  2888	        return null
  2889	    }

  2914	    private fun updateMockDropdownScroll(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  2915	        val dropdown = activeMockDropdownScroll ?: return false
  2916	        if (expandedMockDropdown != dropdown || !mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) return false
  2917	
  2918	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return true
  2919	        val optionHeightPx = (mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stage.scale).coerceAtLeast(1f)
  2920	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockDropdownScrollStartY
  2921	        if (abs(dragDeltaY) > touchSlop) {
  2922	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = true
  2923	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  2924	        }
  2925	
  2926	        if (mockDropdownScrollMoved) {
  2927	            val rowDelta = ((mockDropdownScrollStartY - event.y) / optionHeightPx).roundToInt()
  2928	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, mockDropdownScrollStartOffset + rowDelta)
  2929	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  2930	        }
  2931	        return true
  2932	    }

  2948	    private fun shouldStartMockDragReturn(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  2949	        if (mockDragReturnInProgress || isMockSignInClosing()) return false
  2950	
  2951	        val upwardDragY = mockTouchDownY - event.y
  2952	        if (upwardDragY <= mockBackDragThresholdPx()) return false
  2953	
  2954	        val dragX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  2955	        return upwardDragY > abs(dragX)
  2956	    }

  2960	            MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD * stage.scale
  2961	        } ?: MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD
  2962	        return maxOf(stageThreshold, touchSlop * 3f)
  2963	    }

  3123	    private fun focusMockSignInField(field: MockSignInField) {
  3124	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = field
  3125	        mockInputDimActiveField = field
  3126	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
  3127	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  3128	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()

  3137	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  3138	            inputMethodManager?.restartInput(this)
  3139	            inputMethodManager?.showSoftInput(this, InputMethodManager.SHOW_IMPLICIT)
  3140	        }
  3141	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3142	    }

  3144	    private fun clearMockSignInFieldFocus() {

  3207	        boxWidth: Float
  3208	    ): ForgotRecoverySelectorLayout {
  3209	        val gap = ((width - labelWidth - boxWidth * 2f) / 4f).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  3210	        val labelX = left + gap
  3211	        val usernameBoxX = labelX + labelWidth + gap
  3212	        val passwordBoxX = usernameBoxX + boxWidth + gap

  3214	            labelX = labelX,
  3215	            usernameBoxX = usernameBoxX,
  3216	            passwordBoxX = passwordBoxX,
  3217	            boxWidth = boxWidth
  3218	        )

  3400	    private fun mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  3401	        return (mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size - mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)).coerceAtLeast(0)

  3420	        val selectedIndex = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).indexOf(mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown))
  3421	        setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, if (selectedIndex >= 0) selectedIndex else 0)
  3422	    }

  3546	    private fun startMockForgotPasswordSubmission() {

  3556	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()

  3559	    private fun mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress(): Float {
  3560	        val startMillis = forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis ?: return if (
  3561	            forgotPasswordSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  3562	        ) {
  3563	            1f
  3564	        } else {
  3565	            0f
  3566	        }
  3567	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  3568	        return (elapsed / MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SUBMIT_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  3569	    }

  3573	    }

  3575	    private fun finishMockForgotPasswordSubmissionIfNeeded() {
  3576	        val startMillis = forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis ?: return
  3577	        if (forgotPasswordSubmissionState != MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading) {
  3578	            forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = null
  3579	            return
  3580	        }
  3581	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SUBMIT_MS) return
  3582	
  3583	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  3584	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = null
  3585	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  3586	    }

  3590	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Idle
  3591	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = null
  3592	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  3593	    }

  3595	    private fun startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(
  3596	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  3597	        startProgress: Float = 0f
  3598	    ) {
  3599	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount || isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()) return
  3600	
  3601	        val nextIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndexForDirection(direction)
  3602	        if (nextIndex == mockCreateAvatarIndex) return
  3603	
  3604	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  3605	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
  3606	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex = nextIndex
  3607	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection = direction
  3608	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartProgress = startProgress.coerceIn(0f, 0.96f)
  3609	        mockCreateAvatarIndex = nextIndex
  3610	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  3611	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  3612	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3613	    }
  3614	
  3615	    private fun mockCreateAvatarIndexForDirection(direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection): Int {
  3616	        return (mockCreateAvatarIndex + direction.indexDelta + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT) % MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT
  3617	    }
  3618	
  3619	    private fun mockCreateAvatarDragDirection(): MockAvatarCarouselDirection? {
  3620	        return when {
  3621	            mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX < 0f -> MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next
  3622	            mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX > 0f -> MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous
  3623	            else -> null
  3624	        }
  3625	    }
  3626	
  3627	    private fun mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(): Float {
  3628	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return 0f
  3629	        val scaledAvatarSize = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE
  3630	        return mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(scaledAvatarSize), stage.scale)
  3631	    }
  3632	
  3633	    private fun mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(slideDistance: Float, stageScale: Float): Float {
  3634	        val slideDistancePx = (slideDistance * stageScale).coerceAtLeast(1f)
  3635	        return (abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) / slideDistancePx).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  3636	    }

  3638	    private fun mockCreateAvatarBitmap(index: Int): Bitmap {
  3639	        return when (index.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)) {
  3640	            0 -> steveCreateAvatarCharacter
  3641	            1 -> martinCreateAvatarCharacter
  3642	            else -> jannyCreateAvatarCharacter
  3643	        }
  3644	    }
  3645	
  3646	    private fun mockCreateAvatarCarouselProgress(): Float {
  3647	        val startMillis = mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartMillis ?: return 1f
  3648	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  3649	        val elapsedProgress = (elapsed / MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CAROUSEL_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  3650	        return lerpFloat(mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartProgress, 1f, elapsedProgress).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  3651	    }
  3652	
  3653	    private fun isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating(): Boolean {
  3654	        return mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartMillis != null
  3655	    }
  3656	
  3657	    private fun finishMockCreateAvatarCarouselIfNeeded() {
  3658	        val startMillis = mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartMillis ?: return
  3659	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CAROUSEL_MS) return
  3660	
  3661	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartMillis = null
  3662	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex

  3783	    private fun isSoftKeyboardVisible(): Boolean {
  3784	        val rootInsets = ViewCompat.getRootWindowInsets(this)
  3785	        if (rootInsets != null) {
  3786	            val imeInsets = rootInsets.getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime())
  3787	            if (rootInsets.isVisible(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime()) || imeInsets.bottom > 0) return true
  3788	        }
  3789	
  3790	        val rootHeight = rootView?.height ?: 0
  3791	        if (rootHeight <= 0) return false
  3792	        getWindowVisibleDisplayFrame(tempWindowRect)
  3793	        val obscuredHeight = rootHeight - tempWindowRect.height()
  3794	        return obscuredHeight > rootHeight * 0.18f
  3795	    }

  3906	    private fun appendMockSignInInput(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  3907	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  3908	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  3909	        if (input.isEmpty()) return true
  3910	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  3911	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  3912	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  3913	        val insertedText = input.take(availableChars)
  3914	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + insertedText)
  3915	        activeComposingText = ""
  3916	        return true
  3917	    }
  3918	
  3919	    private fun replaceMockSignInComposingText(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  3920	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  3921	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  3922	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  3923	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  3924	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  3925	        val composingText = input.take(availableChars)
  3926	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + composingText)
  3927	        activeComposingText = composingText
  3928	        return true
  3929	    }
  3930	
  3931	    private fun finishMockSignInComposingText(): Boolean {
  3932	        activeComposingText = ""
  3933	        return true
  3934	    }

  3936	    private fun deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength: Int): Boolean {
  3937	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  3938	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  3939	        if (currentText.isEmpty()) {
  3940	            activeComposingText = ""
  3941	            return true
  3942	        }
  3943	        val deleteCount = beforeLength.coerceAtLeast(1).coerceAtMost(currentText.length)
  3944	        activeComposingText = ""
  3945	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, currentText.dropLast(deleteCount))
  3946	        return true
  3947	    }

  3949	    private fun handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  3950	        activeComposingText = ""
  3951	        val fields = activeMockFields()
  3952	        val fieldIndex = fields.indexOf(focusedMockField)
  3953	        if (fieldIndex >= 0 && fieldIndex < fields.lastIndex && actionCode != EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE) {
  3954	            focusMockSignInField(fields[fieldIndex + 1])
  3955	        } else if (
  3956	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  3957	            focusedMockField == MockSignInField.Email &&
  3958	            mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  3959	        ) {
  3960	            startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  3961	        } else {
  3962	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  3963	            inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
  3964	            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  3965	        }
  3966	        return true
  3967	    }
  3968	
  3969	    private fun handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  3970	        if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  3971	        return when (keyCode) {
  3972	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  3973	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  3974	            else -> {
  3975	                val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  3976	                typedCharacter?.let(::appendMockSignInInput) ?: false
  3977	            }
  3978	        }
  3979	    }

  3981	    private fun sanitizeMockSignInInput(input: String): String {
  3982	        return buildString {
  3983	            input.forEach { character ->
  3984	                if (character != '\n' && character != '\r' && !character.isISOControl()) {
  3985	                    append(character)
  3986	                }
  3987	            }
  3988	        }
  3989	    }
  3990	
  3991	    private fun trailingFittingText(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): String {
  3992	        if (paint.measureText(text) <= maxWidth) return text
  3993	
  3994	        var startIndex = 0
  3995	        while (startIndex < text.length && paint.measureText(text.substring(startIndex)) > maxWidth) {
  3996	            startIndex += 1
  3997	        }
  3998	        return text.substring(startIndex)
  3999	    }
  4000	
  4001	    private fun wrappedTextLines(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): List<String> {
  4002	        val lines = mutableListOf<String>()
  4003	        text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n")).forEachIndexed { paragraphIndex, paragraph ->
  4004	            if (paragraphIndex > 0) {
  4005	                lines += ""
  4006	            }
  4007	
  4008	            var currentLine = ""
  4009	            paragraph.replace('\n', ' ')
  4010	                .split(Regex("\\s+"))
  4011	                .filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
  4012	                .forEach { word ->
  4013	                    val candidateLine = if (currentLine.isEmpty()) word else "$currentLine $word"
  4014	                    if (currentLine.isEmpty() || paint.measureText(candidateLine) <= maxWidth) {
  4015	                        currentLine = candidateLine
  4016	                    } else {
  4017	                        lines += currentLine
  4018	                        currentLine = word
  4019	                    }
  4020	                }
  4021	            if (currentLine.isNotEmpty()) {
  4022	                lines += currentLine
  4023	            }
  4024	        }
  4025	        return lines
  4026	    }
  4027	
  4028	    private fun moveToward(current: Float, target: Float, amount: Float): Float {
  4029	        return when {
  4030	            current < target -> (current + amount).coerceAtMost(target)
  4031	            current > target -> (current - amount).coerceAtLeast(target)
  4032	            else -> current
  4033	        }
  4034	    }

  4039	        val stageScale = min(width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  4040	        return StageMetrics(
  4041	            left = (width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f,
  4042	            top = (height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * stageScale) / 2f +
  4043	                mockAuthOpenStageLiftY(stageScale) +
  4044	                mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale),
  4045	            scale = stageScale
  4046	        )
  4047	    }

  4110	    private fun switchMockAccountFlow(flow: MockAccountFlow) {
  4111	        if (
  4112	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  4113	            activeMockFlow == flow ||
  4114	            isMockSignInClosing() ||
  4115	            isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()
  4116	        ) {
  4117	            return
  4118	        }
  4119	
  4120	        val previousFlow = activeMockFlow ?: return
  4121	        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  4122	        activeComposingText = ""
  4123	        expandedMockDropdown = null
  4124	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  4125	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  4126	        pressedMockField = null
  4127	        pressedRememberMe = false
  4128	        pressedBackChevron = false
  4129	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  4130	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  4131	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  4132	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  4133	        pressedMockDropdown = null
  4134	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  4135	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  4136	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  4137	        resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()
  4138	        if (flow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  4139	            forgotRecoveryTarget = MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password
  4140	            emailText = ""
  4141	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  4142	        }
  4143	        outgoingMockFlow = previousFlow
  4144	        activeMockFlow = flow
  4145	        mockFlowSwitchStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  4146	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  4147	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  4148	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  4149	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()

  4151	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()

  4188	    }
  4189

  4191	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null) return

  4237	    private fun mockSignInProgress(): Float {
  4238	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return 0f
  4239	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  4240	        val progress = (elapsed / mockSignInTransitionDurationMs().toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4241	        return lerpFloat(
  4242	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress,
  4243	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress,
  4244	            progress
  4245	        )
  4246	    }
  4247	
  4248	    private fun mockFlowSwitchProgress(): Float {
  4249	        val startMillis = mockFlowSwitchStartMillis ?: return 1f
  4250	        return ((SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis) / MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS.toFloat())
  4251	            .coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4252	    }
  4253	
  4254	    private fun isMockFlowSwitchAnimating(): Boolean {
  4255	        return mockFlowSwitchStartMillis != null && mockFlowSwitchProgress() < 1f
  4256	    }
  4257	
  4258	    private fun finishMockFlowSwitchIfNeeded() {
  4259	        if (mockFlowSwitchStartMillis == null || mockFlowSwitchProgress() < 1f) return
  4260	        outgoingMockFlow = null
  4261	        mockFlowSwitchStartMillis = null
  4262	    }
  4263	
  4264	    private fun isMockSignInAnimating(): Boolean {
  4265	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return false
  4266	        return SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()
  4267	    }
  4268	
  4269	    private fun isMockSignInClosing(): Boolean {
  4270	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f
  4271	    }
  4272	
  4273	    private fun mockSignInTransitionDurationMs(): Long {
  4274	        val distance = abs(mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress - mockSignInTransitionStartProgress)
  4275	        return (MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS * distance)
  4276	            .roundToInt()
  4277	            .coerceAtLeast(1)
  4278	            .toLong()
  4279	    }
  4280	
  4281	    private fun finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded() {
  4282	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return
  4283	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()) return
  4284	
  4285	        if (mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f) {
  4286	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
  4287	            activeMockFlow = null
  4288	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
  4289	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
  4290	            pressedMockField = null
  4291	            pressedRememberMe = false
  4292	            pressedBackChevron = false
  4293	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  4294	            pressedMockDropdown = null
  4295	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  4296	            pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  4297	            pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  4298	            activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  4299	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  4300	            mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  4301	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  4302	            resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()
  4303	            notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  4304	            return
  4305	        }
  4306	
  4307	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
  4308	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
  4309	    }
  4310	
  4311	    private inner class MockSignInInputConnection : BaseInputConnection(this@CinerificIntroView, false) {
  4312	        override fun commitText(text: CharSequence?, newCursorPosition: Int): Boolean {
  4313	            return appendMockSignInInput(text?.toString().orEmpty())
  4314	        }
  4315	
  4316	        override fun setComposingText(text: CharSequence?, newCursorPosition: Int): Boolean {
  4317	            return replaceMockSignInComposingText(text?.toString().orEmpty())
  4318	        }
  4319	
  4320	        override fun finishComposingText(): Boolean {
  4321	            return finishMockSignInComposingText()
  4322	        }
  4323	
  4324	        override fun deleteSurroundingText(beforeLength: Int, afterLength: Int): Boolean {
  4325	            return deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength)
  4326	        }
  4327	
  4328	        override fun deleteSurroundingTextInCodePoints(beforeLength: Int, afterLength: Int): Boolean {
  4329	            return deleteSurroundingText(beforeLength, afterLength)
  4330	        }
  4331	
  4332	        override fun getTextBeforeCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {
  4333	            val text = focusedMockField?.let(::mockSignInFieldText).orEmpty()
  4334	            return text.takeLast(n.coerceAtLeast(0))
  4335	        }
  4336	
  4337	        override fun getTextAfterCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {
  4338	            return ""
  4339	        }
  4340	
  4341	        override fun getSelectedText(flags: Int): CharSequence {
  4342	            return ""
  4343	        }
  4344	
  4345	        override fun performEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  4346	            return handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode)
  4347	        }
  4348	
  4349	        override fun sendKeyEvent(event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  4350	            if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  4351	            return when (event.keyCode) {
  4352	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  4353	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  4354	                else -> {
  4355	                    val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  4356	                    if (typedCharacter != null) {
  4357	                        appendMockSignInInput(typedCharacter)
  4358	                    } else {
  4359	                        super.sendKeyEvent(event)
  4360	                    }
  4361	                }
  4362	            }
  4363	        }
  4364	    }
  4365	}

  4520	private inline fun <reified T : Enum<T>> enumValueOrNull(name: String): T? {
  4521	    return runCatching { enumValueOf<T>(name) }.getOrNull()
  4522	}
  4523	
  4524	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  4525	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  4526	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4527	}
  4528	
  4529	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  4530	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  4531	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4532	}
  4533	
  4534	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  4535	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  4536	}
  4537	
  4538	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  4539	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  4540	}
  4541	
  4542	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  4543	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4544	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  4545	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  4546	    return base + bounce
  4547	}
  4548	
  4549	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  4550	
  4551	private fun lerpBounds(start: Bounds, end: Bounds, amount: Float) = Bounds(
  4552	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  4553	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  4554	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  4555	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  4556	)
  4557	
  4558	private fun offsetBounds(bounds: Bounds, yOffset: Float) = Bounds(
  4559	    x = bounds.x,
  4560	    y = bounds.y + yOffset,
  4561	    w = bounds.w,
  4562	    h = bounds.h
  4563	)
  4564	
  4565	private fun lerpColor(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  4566	    val t = amount.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4567	    return Color.argb(
  4568	        lerpInt(Color.alpha(start), Color.alpha(end), t),
  4569	        lerpInt(Color.red(start), Color.red(end), t),
  4570	        lerpInt(Color.green(start), Color.green(end), t),
  4571	        lerpInt(Color.blue(start), Color.blue(end), t)
  4572	    )
  4573	}
  4574	
  4575	private fun lerpInt(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  4576	    return (start + (end - start) * amount).roundToInt()
  4577	}
  4578	
  4579	private fun combineBitmaps(base: Bitmap, overlay: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  4580	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(base.width, base.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  4581	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  4582	    val paint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  4583	    canvas.drawBitmap(base, 0f, 0f, paint)
  4584	    canvas.drawBitmap(overlay, 0f, 0f, paint)
  4585	    return output
  4586	}
  4587	
  4588	private fun circularBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  4589	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(source.width, source.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  4590	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  4591	    val rect = RectF(0f, 0f, source.width.toFloat(), source.height.toFloat())
  4592	    val maskPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)
  4593	    val imagePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  4594	    canvas.drawOval(rect, maskPaint)
  4595	    imagePaint.xfermode = PorterDuffXfermode(PorterDuff.Mode.SRC_IN)
  4596	    canvas.drawBitmap(source, null, rect, imagePaint)
  4597	    imagePaint.xfermode = null
  4598	    return output
  4599	}

============================================================
FILE: README.md
============================================================

     1	# Cinerific
     2	
     3	Local Android project for the `prahlin/cinerific` GitHub repo.
     4	
     5	This is a first Kotlin / Jetpack Compose scaffold based on the Figma intro and sign-in frames. The current app opens as a tablet-friendly full-screen frame preview with:
     6	
     7	- Intro frame 1
     8	- Animated intro frame 2
     9	- Promo/logo intro frame 3
    10	- Sign-in/loading frame 4
    11	
    12	## Stack
    13	
    14	- Kotlin
    15	- Jetpack Compose Material 3
    16	- Android Gradle Plugin 8.5.x
    17	- Gradle 8.7
    18	
    19	## Run it
    20	
    21	Open the project in Android Studio and run `app`, or use the Gradle wrapper:
    22	
    23	```bash
    24	./gradlew :app:assembleDebug
    25	```
    26	
    27	The project pins Gradle to Android Studio's bundled JBR in `gradle.properties`, so command-line builds do not accidentally use an unsupported system JDK.
    28	
    29	From VS Code, run the default build task `Android: assemble debug`. There are also tasks for `Android: install debug` and `Android: launch Cinerific` when an emulator or device is connected.
    30	
    31	## Notes for the first Kotlin pass
    32	
    33	- The layout is intentionally simple and adaptive for tablets.
    34	- The current visuals are Compose approximations of the Figma frames and are meant to be replaced with production assets screen by screen.
    35	- The local project folder is the same path as the GitHub remote: `prahlin/cinerific`.

============================================================
FILE: read_project.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	
     3	PROJECT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
     4	OUTPUT_FILE="$PROJECT_DIR/cinerific_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt"
     5	
     6	echo "Generating Cinerific project snapshot..."
     7	echo "Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR"
     8	echo "Snapshot: $OUTPUT_FILE"
     9	echo ""
    10	
    11	write_header() {
    12	  cat > "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
    13	Cinerific Android PROJECT SNAPSHOT
    14	Generated: $(date)
    15	Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR
    16	
    17	IMPORTANT INSTRUCTIONS FOR CHATGPT & CODEX:
    18	1. Read this entire snapshot before giving code edits.
    19	2. Preserve all assets, spacing, alignment, fonts, images, layout, animations, and relative positioning unless specifically asked to change them.
    20	3. Do not remove unrelated code.
    21	4. Do not rewrite large sections unless requested.
    22	5. When giving code changes, include exact line/row numbers whenever possible.
    23	6. Print full scripts when requested.
    24	7. Treat this project structure as the source of truth.
    25	8. If you don't understand something, ask for clarification instead of making assumptions.
    26	9. Warn me, or at least caution me, if I ask for something that would break the project or cause it to not work as intended.
    27	
    28	
    29	============================================================
    30	SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
    31	============================================================
    32	
    33	This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
    34	Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.
    35	
    36	The snapshot intentionally ignores:
    37	- .git
    38	- .gradle
    39	- .idea
    40	- app/build
    41	- build
    42	- .kotlin
    43	- .tmp
    44	- .cache
    45	- .DS_Store
    46	- local.properties
    47	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
    48	- *.jks
    49	- *.keystore
    50	
    51	Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.
    52	
    53	
    54	============================================================
    55	PROJECT FILE TREE
    56	============================================================
    57	
    58	EOF
    59	}
    60	
    61	print_file() {
    62	  local file="$1"
    63	
    64	  if [ -f "$file" ]; then
    65	    {
    66	      echo ""
    67	      echo ""
    68	      echo "============================================================"
    69	      echo "FILE: ${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}"
    70	      echo "============================================================"
    71	      echo ""
    72	      nl -ba "$file"
    73	    } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    74	  fi
    75	}
    76	
    77	print_binary_inventory() {
    78	  local title="$1"
    79	  local dir="$2"
    80	
    81	  {
    82	    echo ""
    83	    echo ""
    84	    echo "============================================================"
    85	    echo "$title"
    86	    echo "============================================================"
    87	    echo ""
    88	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    89	
    90	  if [ ! -d "$dir" ]; then
    91	    echo "Directory not present: ${dir#$PROJECT_DIR/}" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    92	    return
    93	  fi
    94	
    95	  find "$dir" -type f \
    96	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
    97	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
    98	    ! -name "*.jks" \
    99	    ! -name "*.keystore" \
   100	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   101	    local size
   102	    local modified
   103	    size="$(stat -f "%z" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%s" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   104	    modified="$(stat -f "%Sm" -t "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%y" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   105	    printf '%s | %s bytes | modified %s\n' "${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}" "$size" "$modified" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   106	  done
   107	}
   108	
   109	write_snapshot() {
   110	  write_header
   111	
   112	  find "$PROJECT_DIR" \
   113	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.git" -prune -o \
   114	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.gradle" -prune -o \
   115	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.idea" -prune -o \
   116	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/app/build" -prune -o \
   117	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/build" -prune -o \
   118	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.kotlin" -prune -o \
   119	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.tmp" -prune -o \
   120	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.cache" -prune -o \
   121	    -name ".DS_Store" -prune -o \
   122	    -name "local.properties" -prune -o \
   123	    -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" -prune -o \
   124	    -name "*.jks" -prune -o \
   125	    -name "*.keystore" -prune -o \
   126	    -print | sort >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   127	
   128	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   129	
   130	
   131	============================================================
   132	FILE CONTENTS — ANDROID SOURCE
   133	============================================================
   134	
   135	EOF
   136	
   137	  for file in \
   138	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml" \
   139	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/MainActivity.kt" \
   140	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificApp.kt" \
   141	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificIntroView.kt" \
   142	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/theme/Theme.kt" \
   143	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml" \
   144	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values/themes.xml" \
   145	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values-night/themes.xml" \
   146	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values-v31/themes.xml" \
   147	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/splash_transparent.xml" \
   148	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_launcher_background.xml" \
   149	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_launcher_foreground.xml" \
   150	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher.xml" \
   151	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher_round.xml"
   152	  do
   153	    print_file "$file"
   154	  done
   155	
   156	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   157	
   158	
   159	============================================================
   160	FILE CONTENTS — ROOT / TOOLING
   161	============================================================
   162	
   163	EOF
   164	
   165	  for file in \
   166	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.gitignore" \
   167	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.github/copilot-instructions.md" \
   168	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.vscode/mcp.json" \
   169	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.vscode/tasks.json" \
   170	    "$PROJECT_DIR/README.md" \
   171	    "$PROJECT_DIR/build.gradle.kts" \
   172	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/build.gradle.kts" \
   173	    "$PROJECT_DIR/settings.gradle.kts" \
   174	    "$PROJECT_DIR/gradle.properties" \
   175	    "$PROJECT_DIR/gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.properties" \
   176	    "$PROJECT_DIR/read_project.sh"
   177	  do
   178	    print_file "$file"
   179	  done
   180	
   181	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / IMAGE ASSET INVENTORY — DRAWABLE-NODPI" "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable-nodpi"
   182	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / SCREENSHOT INVENTORY" "$PROJECT_DIR/screenshots"
   183	
   184	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   185	
   186	
   187	============================================================
   188	NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
   189	============================================================
   190	
   191	The Cinerific snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private directories:
   192	- .git
   193	- .gradle
   194	- .idea
   195	- app/build
   196	- build
   197	- .kotlin
   198	- .tmp
   199	- .cache
   200	- .DS_Store
   201	- local.properties
   202	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
   203	- *.jks
   204	- *.keystore
   205	
   206	The snapshot lists binary assets and screenshots by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF bytes.
   207	
   208	EOF
   209	}
   210	
   211	write_snapshot
   212	
   213	echo "Done."
   214	echo ""
   215	echo "Snapshot created here:"
   216	echo "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   217	echo ""
   218	echo "Open it with:"
   219	echo "open \"$OUTPUT_FILE\""

============================================================
NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
============================================================

The Cinerific snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private directories:
- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.jks
- *.keystore

The snapshot lists binary assets and screenshots by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF bytes.
